tēciya ilakkiyak koḷki iṟukkamāṉa araciyal cārpi veḷikkāṭṭātatāl potuvāka ellōrālum atu ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. āṉāl muṟpōkku ilakkiyak koḷki tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa araciyal cārpi uḷḷaṭakkuvataṉāl ataṟku etirāṉa ilakkiyak kōṭpāṭukaḷ uruvākuvataṟku atu vaḻikōliyatu. anta vakiyil 1960 kaḷiṉ piṟpakutiyil īḻattu vimarcaṉa ulakil iraṇṭu putiya ilakkiyak koḷkikaḷ muṉvikkap paṭṭaṉa.
oṉṟu naṟpōkku ilakkiyam, maṟṟatu pirapañca yatārtta vātam.

naṟpōkku ilakkiyak koḷkiyi muṉvittavar es. poṉṉutturiyāvar. 1950 kaḷil muṟpōkku eḻuttāḷar caṅkattuṭaṉ iṇintu niṉṟu 'makkaḷ ilakkiya'tti ātarittup putumi ilakkiyam itaḻil kaṭṭuri eḻutiya es.po. 60 kaḷil muṟpōkku ilakkiyattiṉ piratāṉa etir vimarcakarkaḷuḷ oruvarāka māṟiṉār. muṟpōkku eṉṟa collukku etirāka naṟpōkku eṉpatip payaṉpaṭuttiyarit tavira tiṭṭamāṉa
karuttukkaḷ etiyum avar muṉvikkavilli. ākavē īḻattil ivarāl maṭṭumē muṉvikkappaṭṭa oru ilakkiyak
kōcamāka atu maṟintatu. ciṟanta paṭippāḷiyāṉa es.po. cila nalla vimarcaṉak kaṭṭurikaḷum eḻutiyavar. eṉiṉum ākkappūrvamāṉa vimarcaṉaṅkaḷi viṭak kaṇṭaṉaṅkaḷilēyē ivar atika caktiyic celaviṭṭuḷḷar. pantanūl mūlamum uriyum eṉṟa ivaratu nūl ivaratu vimarcaṉa āḷumi virayamākkappaṭṭataṟkuc ciṟanta utāraṇamākum.

mu. taḷiyaciṅkam pirapañca yatārtta vātam eṉṟa koḷkiyi muṉvittār. muṟpōkku ilakkiyattukku etirāka kāttiramāṉa vimarcaṉak karuttukkaḷi muṉvittavar ivarē. muṟpōkku ilakkiyattiṉ cittānta taḷamāṉa mārksīya tattuvattiyē ivar vimarcaṉattukku uṭpaṭuttiṉār. intiyāvil mārksīyam eṉṟa collik kēṭṭālē āttiramuṟṟu avastippaṭum veṅkaṭ cāmi nātaṉ pōl aṉṟi, mu. taḷiyaciṅkam
mārksīyatti oru tattuvārtta camayavaḻi niṉṟu vimarcittār. mārksīyattiṉ cila pakutikaḷi ēṟṟukkoṇṭu
ataṟku appāl cella vēṇṭum eṉpatē ivaratu karuttākum.

"mārksīyatti maṟukkum teḷivaṟṟa kaliñarkaḷum cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷum mutalil ati uṇarntu oppuk koṇṭāltāṉ avarkaḷiṉ maṟuppu veṟum maṟuppākavē niṉṟuviṭāmal iṉṟu carittiram kāṭṭiniṟkum putiya taruṇatti iṉam kaṇṭu payaṉpaṭutti mārksīyattiyum mīṟi vaḷarum veṟṟiyāka māṟuvataṟku vaḻipiṟakkum. atāvatu anta uṇmiyi oppuk koṇṭāltāṉ anta uṇmiyiṉ aṭutta pakkattiyum uṇaralām" eṉṟu
taḷiyaciṅkam eḻutiyuḷḷār. mēlittēya mārksīya etirppuc cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷiṉ
karuttukkaḷiyum aravintar pōṉṟavarkaḷ piratipalitta intiya āṉmika vātattiyum oṉṟiṇittatē ivaratu koḷkiyākum. avar tamatu ilakkiyak kōṭpāṭu paṟṟi eḻutukiyil 'iṉi varavēṇṭiya kōṭpāṭṭi 'pirañca yatārttam' eṉṟu kūṟalām; viññāṉamum āṉmika ñāṉamum, vāḻkki ākiyavaṟṟuk kiṭiyē pētam irukkātu. vāḻkkiyē kaliyākum. kaliyi aḻikkum kali ilakkiyatti aḻikkum ilakkiyam
iviyē iṉit tōṉṟap pōkiṉṟaṉa. atuvē meyyuḷ eṉpatu ivaratu kali ilakkiyam paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷiṉ
cāramākum, antavakiyil ivar tarkka muraṇ niṟinta āṉmikak kaṟpaṉāvātiyēyāvār. mu.poṉṉampalam, eṉ.kē. makāliṅkam, imiyavaṉ mutaliyōr ivaratu karuttukkaḷiṉ celvākku uṭpaṭṭavarkaḷāvar, taḷiyaciṅkattiṉ pōrppaṟi, meyyuḷ ākiyavi īḻattiṉ oru kuṟippiṭṭa ilakkiyac cintaṉiyi veḷikkāṭṭum mika mukkiyamāṉa nūlkaḷākum. ivaratu ēḻāṇṭu ilakkiya vaḷarcci - avacarak kuṟippukkaḷ
eṉṟa kaṭṭurit toṭarum kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

ituvari nām nōkkiyatil iruntu 1960 ām āṇṭukaḷil ilakkiya vimarcaṉak koḷkikaḷ īḻattil cittānta rītiyil vaḷarcciyuṟṟaṉa eṉpatik kāṇalām. muṟpōkku ilakkiyam, tēciya ilakkiyam eṉṟa kōṭpāṭukaḷ kāttiramāka vaḷarcciyuṟṟatāl araciyal rītiyil ati etirttavarkaḷum cariyākavō, piḻiyākavō tavirkka muṭiyāmal putiya kōṭpāṭukaḷiyē muṉvikka vēṇṭi iruntatu. ataṉ viḷivē naṟpōkku
vātam, pirapañca yatārttam vātam eṉpaṉavākum.

5

ilakkiyattiṉ camutāya uḷḷaṭakkam paṟṟiya kōṭpāṭukaḷum aṇuku muṟikaḷum iṅku vaḷarcciyaṭintatu pōl ilakkiyattiṉ uruvam paṟṟiya vimarcaṉak kōṭpāṭukaḷum īḻattil ikkālap pakutiyil vaḷarcciyaṭintaṉa. ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ, ilakkiyap pākupāṭu, ilakkiya racaṉi eṉpavi paṟṟiya cintaṉikaḷi ivi uḷḷaṭakkiṉa. ivvakiyil kavitik kōṭpāṭukaḷi vakuttuk kūṟum murukiyaṉiṉ oru cila
viti ceyvōm, murukiyaṉum, kilācapatiyum eḻutiya kaviti nayam, ākiya nūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭat
takkaṉa. kilācapatiyiṉ ilakkiyamum tiṉāyvum eṉṉum nūlum ippirivil aṭaṅkakkūṭiyatē. puṉikati vaṭivam paṟṟiya civattampiyiṉ puṉikatiyum katippiṉṉalum eṉṉum kaṭṭuriyum kaviti nāṭakam paṟṟi em.ē. nuḵmāṉ, mu. poṉṉampalam ākiyōr eḻutiya kaṭṭurikaḷum, putuk kavitiyiṉ amippu paṟṟi ciṟipati, capā jeyarācā ākiyōr eḻutiya cila kaṭṭurikaḷum ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya
kuṟippiṭattakka kaṭṭurikaḷākum

ilakkiya vimarcaṉam, ilakkiyak koḷki, ilakkiya varalāṟu ākiyavi taṉittaṉit tuṟikaḷ eṉiṉum avi muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭavi alla. oṉṟi oṉṟu cārntum uḷḷaṭakkiyum celvaṉa. avvakiyil vimarcaṉa pūrvamāṉa ilakkiya varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ cilavum iṅku tōṉṟiṉa. ivvakiyil pērāciriyar vi. celvanāyakattiṉ tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu palarukku ātarcamākavum muṉṉōṭiyākavum amintatu. kalāniti
ā. vēluppiḷḷiyiṉ tamiḻ ilakkiyattiṉ kālamum ilakkiyattiṉ īḻattaṟiñar peru muyaṟcikaḷ, kaṉaka
centinātaṉiṉ īḻattu ilakkiya vaḷarcci, cokkaṉiṉ īḻattu nāṭaka ilakkiya vaḷarcci, cuppiramaṇiya aiyariṉ īḻattut tamiḻ nāval ilakkiyam mutaliya nūlkaḷ ivvakiyil kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa.

taṉippaṭṭa paṭippukkaḷ paṭippāḷikaḷ paṟṟi vimarcaṉa pūrvamāṉa matippīṭukaḷ iṅku perumaḷavu ceyyappaṭavilli eṉiṉum kuṟippiṭattakka cila muyaṟcikaḷ naṭipeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. kilācapatiyiṉ iru makākavikaḷ, civattampiyiṉ tamiḻc ciṟukatiyiṉ tōṟṟamum vaḷarcciyum, tillinātaṉiṉ vaḷḷuvar mutal pāratitācaṉ vari, cempiyaṉ celvaṉiṉ īḻattut tamiḻ ciṟukati maṇikaḷ ākiya nūlkaḷ ivvakiyil
kuṟippiṭat takkaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cempiyaṉ celvaṉiṉ nūlē muṟṟilum īḻattup paṭippāḷikaḷ paṟṟiyatu.
ivvakiyil mahākavi paṟṟi caṇmukam civaliṅkam, em.ē. nuḵmāṉ ākiyōr eḻutiya kaṭṭurikaḷum kuṟippiṭat takkaṉa. taṉippaṭippu eṉṟa vakiyil mahākaviyiṉ cātāraṇa maṉitaṉatu carittiram paṟṟi caṇmukam civaliṅkam eḻutiya virivāṉa vimarcaṉam īḻattu vimarcaṉa muyaṟcikaḷuḷ mika mukkiyamāṉa kavaṉattukkuriya oṉṟākum. ivitavira īḻattil, ilakkiya vimarcaṉattuṟiyil ā.
civanēcaccelvaṉ, kalā paramēsvaraṉ, cittiralēkā, maṉōṉmaṇi caṇmukatās, mu. nittiyāṉantaṉ,
ce. yōkarācā, turi maṉōkaraṉ, ka.caṇmukaliṅkam, es.em.jē. pistīṉ, kē.es. civakumāraṉ, em.ec.em. cams, ci. meḷaṉakuru mutaliyōrum kuṟippiṭattakka muyaṟcikaḷ ceytuḷḷaṉar.

camīpa kālamāka īḻattu ilakkiya vimarcaṉa muyaṟcikaḷil moḻiyil aṟiviṉ celvākkiyum kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu, ilakkiyam moḻiyiṉāl ākum oru kali eṉṟa vakiyil moḻiyil aṟivu ilakkiya vimarcaṉattil naṉku payaṉpaṭa muṭiyum. mēlit tēyaṅkaḷil ittuṟi naṉku vaḷarcciyaṭintuḷḷatu. tamiḻakattil aṇmiyil veḷivanta muttuccaṇmukaṉiṉ ilakkiyak kōṭpāṭu eṉṟa nūlil moḻiyil aṟivu naṉku
payaṉpaṭṭuḷḷatik kāṇalām. īḻattil kalāniti caṇmukatās, em.ē.nuḵmāṉ ākiyōr ittuṟiyil ciṟu muyaṟcikaḷ
ceytuḷḷaṉar. caṇmukatāsiṉ ākka ilakkiyamum moḻiyiyalum, kaviñarum moḻiyum, em.ē. nuḵmāṉiṉ ākka ilakkiyamum naṭiyiyalum, īḻattu nāvalkaḷiṉ moḻi mutaliya kaṭṭurikaḷ ittuṟiyil ārampa muyaṟcikaḷākak karutattakkaṉa.
